# Australian Renewable Energy Agency Project Vocabulary Signatures

For each project, glossary terms that appear at disproportionately high rates vs the corpus base. The project's vocabulary signature.

**Method.** For each project, find glossary terms with ≥2 mentions where the project-share is ≥3.0× the corpus base rate. Top 25 per project.

**Coverage.** 489 projects.

## Projects (by total glossary mentions)
